This file tracks the ongoing tasks for the FieldLogicHQ platform (multi-tenant sports club and league management). AI models and the USER use this to coordinate work.

- [ ] **🎩 Demo sandbox — the mobile hat (2026-08-07, BUILT on `dev`, UNCOMMITTED, owner QA pending)** — the demo's own chrome was 183px on a 390px phone and, stacked with the product's event header and score ticker, gave **42% of an 844px screen to fixed furniture** before a word of product. Rule applied: *one band is the claim and never moves; everything else is guidance, and guidance yields to the product* — so the never-dismissible invariant is intact (it protects the banner, not the tour). Three changes: the banner squares up into two columns (identity over status left, signup door pinned right, status truncates never wraps); the moments dock + tour + narration fold away on scroll below 640px and return on scroll-up **or via a `2/6` handle that only exists while folded** (the 2026-08-06 "nothing could bring it back" defect is one `display:none` away on this surface); and the live score pill stands down where the product's own ticker is already naming the same game, keeping only the seam reading the ticker cannot make. Measured 183→135 at rest→**53 folded** (coach 155→139→53), desktop unchanged, 0 reachable tab stops in the fold. New shared `useScrollCollapsed` (64/12 hysteresis; reads the scroll EVENT's target — a fixed bar parented to `<body>` cannot walk up into the shell's scroller). ⬜ **Follow-up owed:** `AdminEventHeader` + `CoachTeamHeader` still carry the same reading logic inline, so the 64/12 threshold pair now lives in THREE places — deliberately not migrated in a demo-chrome diff (they collapse-in-place, this folds away, so it is not a mechanical swap), but a fix to the hysteresis maths would silently miss them. ⚠ **One copy change needs ratifying:** the coach claim shortens to "Nothing is saved." on phones only, because the full sentence was ellipsising mid-word in the narrower column. (see docs/projects/active/DEMO_SANDBOX_MOBILE_CHROME_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)

- [ ] **💵 Coach Money hub — tabbed navigation (BUILT + owner QA PASSED 2026-08-11, on `dev`)** — Money's 7 screens (Budget Plan, Player Dues, Fundraisers, Expenses & Payables, Org Allocations, Payment Requests, Budget vs. Actual) each used to be a one-way full-page navigation; a persistent tab bar (Overview + one per section) now swaps them in place on desktop and phone. (The old direct URLs initially kept serving tab-less standalone pages — retired 2026-08-13, see the legacy-routes entry below: they are permanent redirects into the hub now.) Panels are code-split and stay mounted once visited, so an in-progress form survives a tab switch. ✅ `/simplify` (4 lenses) + `/review` (high-risk tier, 4 lenses) + `/docs` all run pre-commit. **The review earned its keep — 6 real defects found and fixed, none visible in casual clicking:** a deep link/refresh into any non-Overview tab rendered a blank pane; a dirty form on a background tab hijacked every subsequent click app-wide with a phantom unsaved-changes prompt (a shared-component assumption this feature broke — `UnsavedChangesGuard` now separates click-interception from the tab-close warning); switching seasons silently dropped the active tab; the one-shot deep-link CTAs (`?starter=`/`?generate=`/`?tab=`) fired only on a panel's FIRST ever mount and no-oped forever after; an org-only tab could dead-end blank after access changed; and stale one-shot params leaked across unrelated tab switches. ⚠ **Two rendered-only defects the file-reading gates could never have caught, both from `check:layout`:** the tab row failed the 44px tap floor and scrolled the whole document sideways by 2px, and later the scroll-arrow buttons rendered **8px tall** because the row's margins collapsed through their padding-less wrapper. ⚠ **Follow-up owed:** the "hide chrome when embedded" mechanism is a per-panel boolean copied 7 times rather than one shared wrapper — fine for one hub, worth extracting if a second tabbed hub appears. Also **found while here:** two Money screens carried a *stale local copy* of the shared page-header rule, which is why their action buttons sat left while the other five sat right — the copies are now reconciled. (see docs/projects/active/COACH_MONEY_HUB_TABS_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)

"Everything in Money" now folds into **two columns** on a wide card (Plan+Collect | Spend+Review, order preserved in the markup so reading and tab order still follow the season), measured **464px → 277px** with the whole list above a 1440×900 fold; group hairlines drop in that state (across two columns "first group" stops meaning "top of the list") and the step markers darken one shade so they stop reading as siblings of the card's own eyebrow. ⚠ **The trigger is the CARD's width, not the viewport, and a media query here would have been a bug** — the same component gets ~1156px on the setup/archived Overview and ~362px in the live dashboard's slot beside the ledger *on the same screen*, so the in-season rail is spared by construction rather than by a remembered exception. ⚠ **The approved mouse-only 34px row floor was deliberately NOT built and the amendment was disclosed:** `(pointer: fine)` does not mean "no finger" (a touchscreen laptop reports a mouse), it bought only ~40px of a 187px win, and it would have put seven new sub-floor entries into the rendered tap-target baseline. In-app Money help corrected in the same pass ("reading DOWN the list is your season in order" was true in one column, half-true in two). typecheck ✓ · token/contrast guardrails ✓ · rendered `check:layout` all 28 coach screens, no new findings ✓ · **plus a Playwright probe reading computed styles and geometry at 1440/1024/768/390 — because a layout change that silently no-ops passes every other gate** (confirmed: `column-count: 2` and two row x-positions at 1440, one column at all three narrower widths, 44px rows throughout). Binding mockup: `claude.ai/code/artifact/b4f5898b-0202-4379-9763-ee080fb1f0b2` (capped-measure alternative recorded as rejected). ✅ **`/review` (standard tier, 3 lenses) run and it EARNED ITS KEEP — 1 High + 1 Low confirmed and fixed, plus 2 advisories documented.** The High was a real defect on real hardware that no single-width check could see: **the portal's own sidebar appears around 940px and takes width back, so the rail's card is WIDER at a 900px window than at a 1000px one** — the first threshold (720px of card width) crossed that non-monotonic curve three times, so the list folded, unfolded and folded again while a window was dragged. Worse, it fired in the ~788–860 band where the in-season row has stacked and the LIVE operate rail is full-width too, giving that rail two ~354px columns on an iPad Pro portrait — so the "the live dashboard is spared" guarantee was false in exactly that band. Two lenses reached the same band independently (one by arithmetic across three stylesheets, one by measurement) and agreed to within 2px. **Threshold raised to 940px**, which the no-sidebar band can never reach (it tops out at 832) and the sidebar band only reaches at ~1260px viewport: measured monotonic afterwards — one column at 390/768/800/834/860/900/940/1024/1100/1180/1240, two at 1280/1440/1536, one switch on the way down, every folded column ≥454px. The operate rail now never folds at any width. Low: the labelled groups' `:last-child` margin reset was DOM-order-keyed inside a layout that fragments by COLUMN, leaving the two columns asymmetric at their feet — reset dropped. Advisories recorded in place: `container-type` silently makes the card a containing block for absolutely-positioned descendants (inert today, a trap for the first tooltip added inside the rail), and a labelled group is atomic in the two-column state so a future all-optional step could leave column two empty. ⚠ **`@container` is this codebase's FIRST container query and the production build uses webpack + cssnano where dev uses Turbopack + Lightning CSS** — a lens flagged its survival as unproven, so it was proven against a real production build rather than reasoned about. ⚠ **The first post-fix `check:layout` ABORTED on the memory floor** — an abort is a failure, and the exit code seen through a pipe is `tail`'s, not the script's; re-run on a fresh dev server. (see docs/projects/active/COACH_MONEY_OVERVIEW_DASHBOARD_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md, docs/projects/active/COACH_MONEY_RAIL_WIDTH_MOCKUP.html)

Owner-raised 2026-08-13; binding mockup `claude.ai/code/artifact/14181bd3-93b2-4cb6-bb11-5f5eb28b14be`. **Delivered:** a shared number column across all five list tables (≈30 inline styles retired; a settled balance drops to muted ink, because a roster where everyone paid was a full column of success green saying nothing); a shared **ledger** replacing Budget Plan's and Budget vs. Actual's two independent category-and-line outlines (column count carried as a CSS variable, so one treatment serves one money column or three); Fundraisers, Payables and Payments converted from hand-built card lists to the shared list table — Fundraisers alone printed its three column headings on every card, the repeated-label rule the Export menu was rejected over twice the day before; Payables' deposit/balance pair moved one click in behind **Payment details** with a derived status pill, since it is the only genuinely nested row in the hub; the two money grids unified on ONE heading recipe (the plan grid's, which carried the reasoned 08-12 size-up — the month grid moved to it and gave the plan grid its sticky heading in return); and the two Dues tables that scrolled sideways in silence given the card-stacking every other list has. ⚠ **THE BIGGER FINDING IS NOT ABOUT TABLES: the rendered layout gate had been sweeping EMPTY STATES.** The UAT fixture had zero budget lines, dues, expenses, payables, fundraisers and payment requests, so every Money screen was green because there was nothing on it to measure — and Fundraisers/Payments/Allocations had no coverage at all (the hub keeps inactive panels at `display:none`, which has zero geometry). The seeder now populates Money and the three standalone routes joined the screen list; **the moment the sweep could see real tables it found four genuine defects**, including a pre-existing AA contrast failure on Budget vs. Actual (`--white-30` remaps to a HAIRLINE token in the warm portal) that `check:text-contrast` cannot see because it reads declared rather than rendered colours. Baseline +181 entries, **every one with a written reason; nothing pruned** — the 42 that "no longer reproduced" under the empty fixture all reproduced again with data, which is exactly the trap. One fix reaches beyond Money on purpose: `.cardActionCell` has promised a "full-touch-target button" since Chunk A and never set a height (28–32px on a phone). ⚠ Wider-portal table drift (roster, schedule, attendance, admin) **noted, deliberately not fixed**; the shared pieces are built to lift there later. ✅ `/docs` run (Money guide + keywords). ✅ demo narration verified still true, `check:demos` passes. ⚠ `verify:changed` fails only on **schema parity** (prod behind dev on migs 230/231 — pre-existing). ⚠ **FOLLOW-UP SAME DAY — Pass 4 had under-delivered and was reported as done:** it unified the two grids’ heading row and pinned column only, and the owner caught it on sight (*"these still look like 2 differently formatted tables"*). Finished with two rulings now in `memory/design_decisions.md` — **a parent row is the LARGEST name in its group** (both screens drew the category smaller than the lines it grouped, inverted from each other) and **every hierarchy in a table is collapsible** (the By-period grid and the Budget Plan List view gained chevrons; closed-set not open-set, so a category added later arrives OPEN). ⚠ **The defect underneath: the month grid was SHOUTING the coach’s own line names** — a global rule styles a bare `<th>` as a column heading, and a row heading is also a `<th>`, so `WINTER DOME BLOCK` rendered in a condensed display face used nowhere else for a coach’s words. Budget Plan’s grid cancelled it locally; the month grid never did — **a local fix for a global rule is the smell**. Also fixed: two minus signs on negatives, and a closing total quieter than the rows it summed. **Negative money stays red** (owner), distinct from expected funding whose minus is arithmetic and stays green. Verified by computed styles, not screenshots. 4 baseline entries pruned — the only ones in the whole pass, and provably fixed. ⚠⚠ **THIRD ROUND — THE SAME MISS A THIRD TIME, NOW GUARDED BY A TEST.** Owner: *"shouldn’t we also be consistent between list and by period?"* The category-outranks-its-lines ruling had been applied to the GRID treatment and not the OUTLINE, so one toggle on Budget Plan turned "Tournaments" into "TOURNAMENTS" and inverted the hierarchy back; the category band was a different colour between the two views too. **The pattern is the real lesson of this project: every time, the fix went to the half being looked at** — heading rows but not bodies, grids but not outlines. A comment asking the next contributor to change both had already failed twice, so the third fix is `tests/unit/money-hierarchy-type-scale.test.ts`: it parses the real stylesheet and fails the build if the outline and the grid disagree on a category/line/total’s size or weight, if a category stops outranking its own lines, or if either returns to small caps. It asserts EQUALITY not values, so the scale stays re-ratifiable. **Verified by BREAKING it** (4 failures) — a green test never shown to fail is not evidence. Suite now 1680/1680. Owner QA rides `OWNER_QA_LEDGER.md` §12. (see docs/projects/active/COACH_MONEY_TABLE_CONSISTENCY_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md; kickoff prompt COACH_MONEY_TABLE_CONSISTENCY_BUILD_PROMPT.md)
- [ ] **🧮 How installment amounts get set (BUILT on dev 2026-08-13, owner QA pending)** — the Generate Installments sheet asked for an amount, threw it away at Preview (re-deriving an even split), then used it at Confirm: a coach typing $150 was shown $800 and would have created $150. **That defect is closed** — the preview now renders exactly what gets written, in every mode. Three ways to set amounts: split the budget evenly, split the season estimate evenly (both less expected funding, each card printing its own arithmetic and per-player result **before** it is chosen), or set the amounts yourself with a running comparison that **warns and never blocks** (short = amber, over = danger since it charges families money they don't owe, exact = a quiet tick). In a split mode the amounts fill themselves and re-cut whenever a date is added, with the rounding remainder on the LAST installment so the parts re-add to the whole (pinned by a test across counts 1–12). **Owner call 1:** three of the sheet's four dead ends (no budget, funding covers the season, $0 estimate) were only ever "there is no number to divide" — they become greyed split cards with reasons while manual stays live and the budget offer survives as a link, so a coach reaching this from Player Dues with no budget is no longer turned away (an empty roster stays the one hard blocker). **Owner call 3:** the sheet's opening paragraph stack trimmed — the cards carry that arithmetic now. **Owner call 2:** the preview table was a **13th** money table the consistency inventory missed (it lives inside a modal, and `482a2b19` shipped without it) — folded onto the shared number cell here, plus a Total column, a team-total row, date-only headers (`#1/#2` dropped), the shortfall carried forward onto the confirm step, and a latent phantom-column bug fixed. ⚠ Deliberately OUT of scope: changing dues after a player has paid (rides with the payment-record project) and a budget-page "money in" block (drawn and **withdrawn by the owner** — but the parked finding stands: **player dues can never be an expected-funding line**, since funding is subtracted on the way to "funded by players" and a dues line drives per-player to $0.00). ⚠ Shared module touched — **dev server restart required before browser testing**. ✅ `/simplify` + `/review` run (high-risk, 5 lenses) — **4 real defects found in the new code and fixed**, the sharpest a **NEGATIVE installment** the split could produce (6¢ over 12 dates → −$0.05) which a preview formatter printing absolute values would have shown as an ordinary "$0.05" before the write refused the whole roster; also a Preview button that could stick permanently disabled, a missing-parameter default that silently re-priced a roster, and a preview that could still promise a schedule the write rejects. ✅ `/docs` run (the FAQ said a budget was **required** — this change makes that false). ✅ Demo: the sandbox's own invitation now replaces the raw string `SandboxReadOnly` on confirm, and the 12U's season estimate was decoupled from its line total so the two even-split cards stop printing the same number (needs `npm run tick:demos`). ⚠ **STILL OWED: owner QA (no ledger entry yet), the rendered layout sweep (never completed — needs a restarted dev server), and the commit.** (see docs/projects/archive/COACH_INSTALLMENT_BASIS_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md; - [ ] **💵 A dues payment is not a record (ALL SIX DECISIONS RULED 2026-08-13; ✅ ALL THREE PASSES + REMINDERS-ROW FOLLOW-UP + /simplify + /review BUILT AND RUN on dev same day — mig 232 dev-only, owner QA = ledger §13, awaiting commit OK)** — ✅ `/simplify` (4 lenses, 8 fixes: ONE remainder definition on coverage, required-paidAmount compiler contract, batched projection collapsing the bulk re-run from ~340 sequential round-trips, shared grouping/cents helpers, N+1 gone). ✅ `/review` (high-risk, 5 lenses, 21→11 confirmed, ALL FIXED) **earned its keep — 2 Criticals + 1 High**: raising dues after an overshoot left a STALE auto-credit double-subtracting (and auto-credits have no delete button — uncorrectable); the per-player Edit-schedule path VANISHED overpaid money (a capping regression vs pre-232) and never re-projected coverage; and the Basic→Premium upgrade still stamped paid_at with NO payment row — those families showed owing everything while BOTH reminder paths skipped them. Root fix: `reconcileOverpaymentCredits` — one symmetric mechanism (create when dues drop below money received, claw back when they rise past it; manual/fundraiser credits never touched) on all four paths, + a WRITE-side guard-test rule banning naked paid_at stamps. Also: Mark Paid regained idempotency via an atomic claim (and now covers the CLICKED installment), schedule-ownership check, voidEntry throws, ledger-creation race fixed, reminder emails HTML-escape names, Ask's family answer quotes remainders. ⚠ Report-only residuals for owner: no-transaction failure windows (accepted, documented). **Follow-on decisions RULED same day (sheet `942aa951`) and BUILT:** 1A Overview budget tile now PAID-ONLY; 3A neutral payment-note example; 4A the coach demo's mid-season team shows a part-paid family ($90 of $120 on the FUTURE instalment — a past-due one broke the pinned two-families-behind story, caught by check:demos on the first seed) + one tour sentence. **Thread 2 deliberately NOT patched:** owner is revamping the refund section — built the review portal instead: `QA Season End U15` in the money lab (dues settled incl. one $50 overpayment, $6,350 of $7,000 spent, fundraiser credits $0–$170 across families) for the owner to reason against before specifying the new model. typecheck ✓ · 1710 tests ✓ · check:demos ✓ · both seeders run on dev.
- [ ] **💸 Coach Money — credits meet the bills, money goes out, the refund sheet derives (MODEL CAPTURED + MOCKUPS APPROVED 2026-08-14; PLAN WRITTEN, NOT STARTED)** — the listen-first kickoff ran and the owner's model grew the scope well past "fix the calculator": **a credit is money the team owes a family**, settled exactly one of three ways — it lowers their remaining bills (fundraising applies **last-bill-first**, so a family that earns $500 is asked for $500 less, and reminders say why), it is **paid out in cash** (the books gain an outbox at last — including a parent reimbursed for the pizza they bought, which counts in the budget but moves no team cash), or it is **handed back at season's end**. Three passes, one model. The refund screen loses its Calculate button and typed pot entirely and becomes a derived settlement sheet that is honest year-round, where each family's refund = their own money owed back + an even share, every row opens to its breakdown, and every row is payable from there (siblings paid once). Owner rulings captured on 10 calls, incl. **a family that still owes gets a share and their debt counts as money coming in** (pot grows by it, their share cancels most of the debt), **anyone left out of the split raises everyone else's share** (never a stranded share), **forgiving a balance counts as that family's share already received** (one rule that handles both "forgive a little, they still get a smaller cheque" and "forgive more than a share, they drop out and the rest divides among nine"), and **when the team can't cover what it owes: say so, share nothing, pro-rate nothing**. ⚠ **TWO VERIFIED FINDINGS THAT MOVED EVERY NUMBER:** the fundraiser flow posts **the full amount raised** to the books (the rebate is a credit, not a deduction), so the $1,575 pot figure in circulation had the rebates removed ONCE and the calculator then subtracted all credits AGAIN — the same $625 out twice; real cash held is **$2,200**, surplus **$1,525**, even share **$152.50 not $90**. And the Collected tile **caps** dues at what was owed, so it silently omits an overpayment the team is physically holding — the pot must read uncapped receipts. ⚠ **THE BUILD'S FIRST ACT IS A REFACTOR, NOT A FEATURE:** nothing owns credit arithmetic — summing credits by player is re-implemented **four times server-side plus once in the UI**, and **no test anywhere exercises any of them** (`rep_season_surplus` has no test at all), so the new three-state rule would otherwise have to be got right five times with no regression net. ⚠ Also found: `money-summary` reports a **second, independent credits number** straight off the fundraiser entries table that will diverge under this model, and `fundraisers/entries` calls a credits-subtracted clamped figure "outstanding" — a different number wearing the shared definition's word. ⚠ The refund screen is **already an archive door** (season-surplus is on the season-aware list) — no allow-list gains an entry, but an archived season must render the record with no payout controls. ⚠ **OPEN, BLOCKS THE MIGRATION:** defaulting existing teams to last-bill-first would change live customers' reminder amounts overnight — recommendation is to backfill existing seasons to today's behaviour and default only new ones. ⚠ Build must not start until the dues-payment-record project (line above) is committed — every pass edits files it owns. (see docs/projects/active/COACH_SEASON_REFUND_REVAMP_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md; binding mockups `claude.ai/code/artifact/eae663d0-56e5-46e9-a2e2-9f7220468be2` / COACH_CREDIT_APPLICATION_MOCKUP.html; kickoff prompt COACH_SEASON_REFUND_REVAMP_KICKOFF_PROMPT.md — its listening phase is complete)

<details><summary>Lineage — the payment-record gap this grew out of (2026-08-13)</summary>

the portal can say what a player OWES but not what they PAID, when, or how much: marking an installment paid takes no amount and posts its FULL value to the team ledger dated the day the coach clicked, so a player paying $100/month against $300 quarterly installments has nowhere to be recorded. **The inventory (16 surfaces, all verified against live code) found the workaround is worse than the gap:** logging the part-payments as credits produces an export row reading "Paid $0.00 · Status Fully paid", a team ledger permanently short the money with no trace, a Season Refund Calculator that schedules the family to be REFUNDED what it thinks they never owed, and a bulk-regenerate guard that doesn't protect them (it keys on the paid stamp, which they never earn). Also surfaced: the Dues screen's own "Outstanding" rail is a mislabeled rolling balance (the canonical figure is fetched and never rendered), and three more inline re-implementations of the shared dues definitions beyond the three already consolidated. **Three workarounds, all broken:** mark it paid (books wrong until the rest arrives), wait and mark later (**the 30- and 7-day reminder waves chase a family for money they are faithfully sending**, and they sit in the Overview's unpaid badge and the "haven't paid anything yet" panel), or record a credit (reduces the rolling balance but not outstanding — the two figures deliberately differ — and posts NOTHING to the ledger, so the money never reaches the team's books). Same root cause as the second owner question that day: dues and payments are the same row, so **dues cannot be changed once a player has paid** — the bulk flow skips them entirely, because replacing the schedule would delete the receipt and orphan a real ledger entry (a Critical already found and fixed 2026-08-13). Proposition drawn, ruled, and Pass 1 built: an installment is a PLAN, a payment is a FACT (mockup `ccc923b8`; ruling 5 simplified by the owner — overpayment auto-credits, no prompt). **Pass 1 ships the receipt book:** `rep_dues_payments` (mig 232, dev; backfill 159 stamps → 159 payments, sums equal to the cent, ledger entries ADOPTED not re-posted), a Record payment sheet (amount · date ARRIVED · method · note — the ledger takes the arrival date), part-paid installments reading "$200.00 of $300.00", Mark Paid reborn as a can't-double-charge shortcut, per-payment remove that voids the ledger line (the undo mark-paid never had), automatic overpayment credits that ride their payment, and the never-paid predicate + digest + Ask + remind-unpaid all reading payment facts — a $100/month family stops being dunned as "no payments yet" everywhere the shared predicate reaches. ⚠ paid_at is now a PROJECTION (stamped-first coverage, pinned by test — the backfill was a zero-visible-change event). **Pass 2 (same day): every reader tells the truth** — Collections tile, BvA card, month-grid Actual (payments bucketed by the month money ARRIVED), Dues-Coming-Due (quotes remainders), Budget tab, roster player profile, refund calculator all on payment facts; overdue now counts the MISSING remainder of a late installment, never its face value; the money-summary's hand-mirrored never-paid predicate (which had drifted — it subtracted credits where the real one doesn't) replaced with the shared one; dues footer "Outstanding" renamed **"Balance owing"** (it always summed the other balance). New `dues-definition-guard` test bans stamp-sum paid-derivations outside the two definition modules — **its first run caught two real offenders the Phase A inventory missed** (the Budget tab's collected figure and the player-profile summary), both fixed; org-allocation surfaces exempted by name (different domain, no payment record). **Pass 3 (same day): the edges** — all three reminder templates chase the REMAINDER with a per-row thank-you for what's arrived (candidates carry `remainingAmount`; zero-remainder rows are never candidates); the bulk dues re-run **stops skipping paying players** — schedules rewrite for everyone, payments re-apply, and money beyond a player's new total auto-credits (`playersSkipped` pinned 0 for old clients; modal copy updated at all three touchpoints); the digest/dashboard due-date line quotes remainders; and — the demo-drift rule earning its keep — **the coach-demo seeder, its nightly re-anchor, and both QA/UAT fixture seeders now put a payment record behind every paid stamp and shift `received_date` with the clock** (a stamp is only a projection; unshifted payments would strand the demo's cash a re-anchor behind its books). `/docs` run: new "Recording the money families send" passage + popular part-payment FAQ; redo-dues FAQ rewritten to payments-kept; stale skip-era search phrases replaced. **Deliberately descoped:** refund credit provenance — the double-payout hazard is closed at the source and balances are payments-based; whether fundraiser rebates belong in refund credit portions is the standing mig-029 product design, an owner call, not a defect. typecheck ✓ · 1705 tests ✓ · sliced check:layout no new findings ✓ · check:demos ✓ · verify:changed fails only schema parity (prod behind on migs 230/231/232 — applies at release). (see docs/projects/active/COACH_DUES_PAYMENT_RECORD_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md; kickoff prompt COACH_DUES_PAYMENT_RECORD_BUILD_PROMPT.md; mockup COACH_DUES_PAYMENT_RECORD_MOCKUP.html)

</details>

Now a two-line **plan card**: Planned costs · Expected fundraising · Player installments, no operators, labels doing the explaining. The third figure carries the season's story — **Estimated** (calculated, per-player + Generate beside it) until dues go out, then the official **Scheduled** total — and the owner's key ruling: **a deliberate over-schedule is a BUFFER stated in ordinary ink, never a red flag** (teams plan buffers; only scheduling *short* of the plan draws amber, with the re-run door beside it). An estimate, when set, IS the Planned costs figure with a caption tracking itemization. Rode along: the kind renamed **"Expected funding" → "Expected fundraising"** everywhere (one shared definition + literals in BvA/exports/Generate modal/help; owner: dues are also funding); **money-in never renders a minus sign** (positive in green — the label is the arithmetic; spreadsheet exports keep their negation so sheets still sum); **fundraising lines carry no category** (the picker was offering "Tournaments" for money coming in; nothing anywhere used it); the ledger's amounts align in **one money column** (fixed actions track — the trailing `auto` sized per-row); rows carry a single pencil, **the whole row opens the editor**, and **Delete moved into the edit form** behind the same confirm — on phones the rows are completely clean (tap to edit; the pencil stays sr-only + un-clips on focus for keyboard/AT). The installments banner and the built-page sample link are gone (sample = empty-states-only, superseding Chunk G's D6). ✅ `/review` (high-risk, 4 lenses) **earned its keep — 9 confirmed, all fixed**: three UAT tests still clicked the removed trash/sample UI (rewritten to the new doors, `sBase` hoisted); hand-set dues made the page contradict its own card (Generate CTA now also checks assessed dues); a dues-only team hid its scheduled money behind the first-run card; clearing a fundraising line's name silently resurrected the old one via the hidden item-name fallback; the period grid had borrowed the card's installments label for an estimate-blind number (now "Costs less fundraising"); plus the focus un-clip, a text-selection click guard, and a Number() belt on the dues sum. **The rendered gate paid for itself:** it caught the card spilling 25px at 768 (now stacks at ≤900) and the new caption links at 20px against the 44px floor (hit-box grown without layout shift) — and removing the trash icons/links **retired 16 old baseline defects, pruned**. One refuted finding worth keeping: the "dues sum concatenates strings" claim was disproven against live QA data before it burned an afternoon. ⚠ The repaired UAT money-smoke spec was rewritten by reading; execute before release. Mockups (binding, 4 rounds): `claude.ai/code/artifact/d37d62e3-970a-48f1-a2f7-d558e65e084d`. No migration; help docs re-synced in the same passes.

- [ ] **🔘 Coach portal page-level actions — the sequel to the header ruling (APPROVED 2026-08-13; PHASE 1 BUILT on dev 2026-08-13, Phases 2–4 open)** — the header ruling said WHERE the actions slot is; this says which buttons go in it, how they're drawn, and which survive a phone. Inventory of all 40 team-hub screens found 10 carrying header actions, Import in four places under four names and weights (Roster's has no header home at all — a real defect: bulk-add is unreachable once one player exists), Export on four screens and missing from nine comparable ones, and — inside the Money hub alone — the same create job drawn lime-filled on two tabs and white-outlined on two others. Twelve rules, four phases. Money's header gains constant `Import ▾ / Export ▾` dataset menus (the tournament admin's Data Tools pattern, brought across) while every create drops into its own tab's existing control row, so the tab bar goes back to pure navigation; a Data Tools *destination* was considered and declined (it moves the button away from the moment of need). ⚠ **Rule 11 removes imports and spreadsheet exports from phone headers and therefore retires the paste-a-block path built precisely because phones have no file picker — the mitigation is mandatory, not optional: every empty state keeps its import offer at 390px.** ⚠ The cap came down from three buttons to two after the Plan templates render was drawn and rejected on sight; two of its three buttons turned out to be the same verb (now one `New template ▾`) and the third a library control that belongs with the list. Binding mockup: `claude.ai/code/artifact/44162825-32ef-4744-90dc-7939ee635e9e`. **Phase 1 (Money hub) BUILT on dev 2026-08-13** — hub-wide `Import ▾ / Export ▾`, all seven tabs' creates down into their own toolbars, creates unified to the lime fill, the three missing exports (budget lines, expenses & payables, fundraisers) written, "Recent imports" backed by migration 231 (**dev only — prod apply rides the next release**), and the empty payables list given the import door rule 11 depends on. **Follow-on 2026-08-13 (owner-directed):** Player Dues lost its budget cross-link banner — it pushed the coach out to a page to do a job that screen can now do in place — and its "Set dues for all players" button re-points at the **shared** Generate Installments modal, so there is one bulk-dues door instead of two divergent ones (the retired form had no preview of what any player would owe). The modal now owns its own budget read, carries the start-with-a-budget nudge as its empty state, and a re-run **replaces** the schedule — except for any player who has already paid something, who is skipped and reported. ⚠ That exception is a real fix, not a nicety: the bulk save it replaces deleted every installment for a player, **paid ones with their accounting entries included**. ✅ `/docs` run (the guide described a manual bulk path that no longer exists; rewritten + a new "can I redo the dues?" FAQ). ✅ `/review` run (high-risk tier, 5 lenses) — **3 Criticals confirmed and fixed, all three introduced by this change**: (1) the rewrite discarded its own write result, so a failure after the delete left a player owing a total with no schedule while the coach was told it worked — the schedule row is now secured BEFORE anything is deleted, every write is checked, and failures come back **named**; (2) the client decided "do dues already exist?" from a flag that counts only budget-generated ones, so a roster with any hand-set schedule dead-ended on a 409 quoting a "replace" option no screen offered — **the server is now the authority** and its refusal carries a code the modal turns into a question with the coach's form intact; (3) the extracted modal lost the visible-tab gate on its unsaved-changes guard, re-opening the app-wide click-hijack the tab-bar review already paid for once — `tabActive` now travels with the modal and both callers pass it. Also fixed: a stale preview response could restore a table the form no longer matched, and confirm had no re-entrancy guard. ⚠ **Two known defects left standing by owner ruling:** the preview table is computed from the budget while the write uses the typed amounts (**pre-existing**, predates this change, deferred to the upcoming modal upgrade), and a replace still overwrites hand-built per-player schedules and relabels them budget-generated (accepted as-is). Owner QA rides `OWNER_QA_LEDGER.md`. **Phases 2–4 (Roster/Schedule → the folds → shared classes + the guard) not started.** (see docs/projects/active/COACH_HEADER_ACTIONS_CONSISTENCY_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md; kickoff prompt COACH_PAGE_ACTIONS_P1_MONEY_BUILD_PROMPT.md)

No migration, no new capability.** ⚠ **The build found three things the plan did not:** (1) **`canManageAwards` was a live hole** — it read `schedule || roster`, and a **helper holds `schedule`**, so the awards API accepted a parent volunteer (nav never offered it; the route is the last line and it was open) — now keyed on record access; (2) **the Insights page had ONE flag doing two jobs** — deciding both whether to *fetch* the attendance report and whether to *draw the door* to it, which after the re-key would have fetched a 403 and reported it as a genuine failure, then drawn a door onto it; (3) **the admin mirror kept a hand-written COPY of the capability shape**, which is precisely why A1 produced no compile error there while every other caller failed loudly — now aliased to the real type so the next grant change breaks the build instead of drifting. **Two tests ADDED** (the stale-key contract for pre-A1 stored bundles, and the assistant-reaches-past-plans-without-notes property the narrowing fix depends on). ⚠ `/release` owes ONE line at promote time (assistants who had the roster hidden gain those sections) — wording in plan §7.5. ✅ **RENDERED LAYOUT CHECK RUN 2026-08-03 (all 28 screens): no new findings.** ⚠ It caught ONE real defect first — a **latent tap-floor bug in the shared help component**: every FAQ row was 40px against the 44px floor, and only cleared it when a question happened to wrap to two lines. A newly-added FAQ was the first to fit on one line and exposed it. Fixed on the row (`min-height`), not by padding copy — which **cleared 357 baselined findings across the help system**. ⚠ **The ratchet can tighten but was NOT pruned**: the script refuses to prune from a partial sweep, and a full 28-screen prune is a deliberate ~20-min run — worth doing as its own task. Was: ✅ **SCOPED + RULED.** Mockups v1 (binding spec): https://claude.ai/code/artifact/1f7c75ac-b7bc-42c7-b4bf-69fe71a70a5a — owner ruled all three 2026-08-03: **§3 = Option A** (duties decide sections, no new grant; Option B drawn and declined) · **standing line STAYS** on the staff card · **change notice = release notes + help guides only** (notifying affected head coaches offered and declined — it advertises a control that never worked). ✅ **Unblocked** — Practice Plans Phase 4 committed `2e3e7e0d`. Implements the 2026-08-03 A1 ruling (names/numbers/positions baseline for everyone with portal access; the Roster Hidden/View switch and the hidden `planPlayerNames` grant both retire; `rosterPii` untouched) **with the helper-recognition finding folded in**. ⚠ **THE RE-AUDIT FOUND THE GRANT WAS DOING TWO JOBS** — "may a name render here" (A1 makes this always yes) **and** "does this SECTION exist for this person" (A1 is **silent**, and retiring the grant with nothing behind it opens the roster page, attendance, development board, Insights and the Overview tiles to a **helper** — a widening, which A1 explicitly says it is not). **Recommended answer: no new grant** — section visibility becomes the union of the record grants that already exist (`hasRecordAccess` = the existing `hasNoTeamRecordAccess` negated), so an assistant holding money sees names beside the amounts (the owner's own stated complaint) while a helper's sections stay shut and self-correct if their bundle widens. ⚠⚠ **TWO SILENT BREAKAGES fixed in the same unit of work:** `staffKindLabel` (the word "Helper" on the staff card — relabels every helper "Assistant") and `hasNoTeamRecordAccess` (the *"This season has finished"* screen — **its loss reopens the season-review door closed by the 2026-08-03 ruling**, looking like an unrelated tidy-up). **No migration, no new capability — the model gets strictly smaller.** ⚠ Owner QA needs a **second signed-in helper account** (the gap Phase 4 left). Standing owner item: verify removing the roster toggle leaves the Contacts & birthdates gate exactly as strong — ✅ family access panel already verified `rosterPii`-gated server-side. - [ ] **Coaching staff screen — layout study (2026-07-31)** — owner-flagged; one message was wrapped in three containers (page header → tinted panel repeating it → a centred floating card). ✅ **BUILT ON DEV (uncommitted)**: panel header retired, one shared left edge, invite becomes a labelled control, the access primer becomes a persistent rail instead of a first-run message that vanished at the first invite. 6 binding rules + 3 real defects fixed en route logged in `memory/design_decisions.md`. ✅ **/review (high-risk, 5 lenses) DONE — 1 Critical + 2 High + 5 Medium confirmed and fixed, 3 refuted.** The Critical was self-inflicted and is now a binding rule: **a UI stating a security posture must READ that posture, never re-describe it** — the new access rail derived "off until you turn it on" from *sensitive-group membership*, but sensitive ≠ off-by-default, so it told head coaches that waivers/team files were locked while every new assistant could already read them. Both lists now derive from the server's own defaults. Rejected the finder's alternative fix (flipping the default) — that silently reverses a June owner ruling. Also fixed: the segmented access controls had **no accessible name at all** (a screen reader heard "Hidden, pressed" with no idea if it governed Roster or Team money) and their focus ring was clipped by their own container; "Saving…"/"Saved" — the only confirmation a money/PII grant landed — was never announced; and retiring the visible section header had also deleted it from the heading outline. ⚠ **Two owner rulings wanted, spun out as their own work (NOT part of this commit):** (1) **LIVE PRIVACY DEFECT, likely on prod** — the Documents permission covers blank team templates AND signed per-player files, and the player-file routes check only that permission, never guardian-PII. A default assistant therefore sees a player's guardian email/phone/DOB/medical notes *redacted* on the player page while the Documents table below it lists and downloads that same player's Medical Consent and Waiver PDFs — the redaction is defeated from the same screen. Recommended fix keeps BOTH June rulings intact: leave the Documents default alone, gate *player-attached* documents behind the guardian-PII permission. (2) the sensitive-group note promises "you'll be asked to confirm before granting these" but only 3 of 6 prompt — **Documents, Internal notes AND Tryouts do not** (Tryouts exposes guardian PII for prospective players, the biggest single gap). Fix the behaviour, not the sentence. Mockups: https://claude.ai/code/artifact/e0090415-0fa6-4b36-97bb-37f84a67e7ee — remaining = owner QA → commit

- [ ] **Click-away/Escape sweep** (spun out of the admin dropdown consolidation by `/simplify`; owner-approved 2026-07-30) — ✅ **BUILT ON DEV 2026-07-30 (uncommitted)** — live scan found **17 implementations across 13 files, not the 12 first reported** (one file, the tournament rules icon picker, was missed entirely by the originating scan). **15 converted.** The user-facing win: **seven panels that could not be closed with Escape now can** — both mobile nav "More" menus, the notification bell, the payee picker, the schedule timeline menu, the rules icon picker and a platform-admin menu. Three finds the plan didn't anticipate: (1) the chat emoji picker was the **only** copy handling IME composition correctly (Escape mid-composition must cancel the composition, not close the panel) — that guard moved *into* the shared hook, so all consumers get it; (2) the payee picker **never removed its click listener** — it attached on mount and stayed for the life of the form, now open-only; (3) the notification bell's portaled panel now passes a real ref instead of a global selector query (needed a small optional prop on the panel). Nested-Escape double-close risk was checked and cleared. **Deliberately left (2):** the chat reaction popovers (no container element — converting means restructuring chat markup, deserves its own approval) and the coach team switcher (another session has that file uncommitted; concurrency, not difficulty). typecheck 0 + focused lint 0 errors + 482 tests + all token/date baselines zero + schema parity 0. ✅ **/simplify (4 lenses) + /review (high-risk, 3 lenses) DONE.** `/simplify` escalated the big one: the discovery scan was **incomplete a second time** — it searched for one click-detection API and missed four panels using another, so the true count is **21 across 17 files**. Those four use the other API *deliberately*: one carries a comment that the API everything else uses (including the shared hook) **does not fire for taps on iOS Safari**, which "left it stuck/unusable". So every converted panel may not dismiss when an iPhone user taps blank space — **pre-existing, not introduced here**, but now fixable in one line instead of twenty. Those four are deliberately NOT converted (it would drag them back onto the broken API). ✅ **iOS FIX DECIDED + BUILT 2026-07-30 (own commit `61e0e4af`, sequenced after the sweep so it reverts independently)** — dismissal moved to the touch-safe signal, and the four hold-outs converted now that it's safe: **19 of 21 implementations now share one dismissal contract.** Trade-off accepted: a panel now also closes when a scroll gesture starts outside it (correct, matches mainstream UI libraries — watch it in QA; fallback is tap-vs-scroll detection at real added complexity). Side effect judged an improvement: clicking a chat reaction now closes an open emoji picker (it previously stayed open only by an accident of event plumbing). ⚠ **UNVERIFIABLE LOCALLY — automated browser testing runs on Chromium, which does not reproduce Safari's behaviour. A green gate is NOT evidence this works; it is unproven until someone taps blank space on a real iPhone.** `/review` found **2 NEW defects in this work, both fixed**: (1) **Escape was a trap door** — it closed panels with focus still inside, dropping keyboard users at the top of the page, plausibly *worse* than the gap this work set out to fix; now the shared behaviour hands focus back to whatever opened the panel; (2) **the payee picker could silently drop a typed payee** — Escape closed the list, typing didn't reopen it, and only a picked option is ever recorded, so the field looked filled while nothing saved. Also logged pre-existing, NOT fixed: the payee picker's options **only respond to a mouse (Enter/Space select nothing)** — a full keyboard blocker deserving its own ticket; no overlay knows which is topmost, so two open overlays both close on one Escape; and the scope picker has **zero consumers** (dead code). ⚠ Framing correction: phones have no Escape key, so the two mobile-nav conversions help hardware-keyboard/assistive-tech users, not typical phone users. Remaining = owner QA → commit (see docs/projects/archive/DISMISS_BEHAVIOUR_SWEEP_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md). Original scope note: with the three named admin panels retired onto the shared hook, **12 more private copies of the same click-away/Escape logic remain**. Seven are mechanically convertible (four menus in the tournament schedule page, the registrations filter, the schedule scope picker, and the shared flip pill — whose own comment admits it mirrors the admin More-menu pattern). **Five are the same shape but missing Escape-to-close entirely**, so converting them closes a real keyboard-accessibility gap rather than just deduplicating: both bottom navs, the platform-admin customer-users menu, the schedule timeline, and the accounting payee combobox. Four are NOT mechanical and need a decision each (chat emoji picker refocuses its trigger and tests two refs; chat reaction popovers have no container ref at all; the coach team switcher refocuses on every close; the notification bell is portaled to `<body>`). Also fold in a pre-existing quirk `/review` surfaced (present in every copy, old and new): a **keyboard**-activated trigger fires a click with no preceding mousedown, so tabbing to a second menu and pressing Enter can leave two menus open at once, after which one Escape closes both — the right altitude to fix that is the shared hook, not one panel. ⚠ Scope note: this spans chat, both bottom navs and platform admin — a materially bigger QA surface than the 3-panel job, which is why it was deliberately left out of it (see docs/projects/archive/COACH_ONBOARDING_QUIET_MODE_PLAN.md §C0 remainder)

Fix: each step is honest about state and **turns the tool on itself, then opens it**; completed steps stop asking; one-interaction reversible exit. ⚠ Voice constraint — free is the consumer **companion**, NOT premium's operator HQ: do **not** port Phase B's three-sentence teaching contract, and leave the free section empty states alone (their light payoff copy is already correct). One-click "Skip all this" degrades to the shared faint Explore line (reversible; Explore untouched as browsable home + permanent tab). Three extractions instead of new copies: shared activation call, shared per-team dismiss hook, shared faint line (both Overview nudges now degrade to ONE thing). **Owner decision 2026-07-29 — audience: scratch teams only** (`history.length === 0`), and the card now survives partial progress so a completed step is actually reachable; tournament-registration teams see **no change**. Help guide reconciled (both drifting sections). ✅ **/simplify (4 lenses) DONE — 7 fixed, 1 rejected, 2 skipped:** reuse lens caught a real warm-theme defect (the "Turn on" button missed the TH-5 override its twin has → near-black `--on-lime` on dark olive); **altitude + simplification both flagged the same depth issue — the four Tier-2 tools were described in THREE places, now one shared catalog `lib/coach-team-tools.ts` feeds the shell, Explore and the panel, with section paths DERIVED not hand-written** (nav output verified byte-identical); activation helper now owns its merge (no more `string[] | null` hazard on callers); one derived row-state replaced three independent checks; `subscribe` hoisted to module scope (was re-subscribing every render). **Rejected:** "drop `router.refresh()`" — it's load-bearing (Next 16's refresh reducer invalidates the segment cache SYNCHRONOUSLY, which is what stops a return to the Overview re-offering "Turn on"); the misleading comment that caused the misread is fixed. **Skipped:** `composes` unification of the twin button CSS (Turbopack non-transitivity needs compiled-output verification) + relocating the dismiss hook to a neutral home (bar is a third structurally-different consumer). ✅ **/review (high-risk tier — two new `lib/` modules + the portal nav shell, 4 lenses) DONE — 3 CONFIRMED + fixed, 2 refuted:** **(1) a step could show a checkmark with NO button and no nav tab** — the tool sub-routes are *not* gated on `activated_features` (verified), so a bookmark/typed URL lets a coach add data while the tool stays off; ranking "done" above "on" rebuilt the exact one-way door this panel exists to close → **being switched on now outranks having data**; **(2) an in-flight activation could yank the coach off a page they'd chosen** (the router outlives the component) → alive-ref guard, applied to Explore too; **(3) a stalled navigation left every button disabled behind a spinner only a reload could clear** → `busy` clears before navigating + a `justActivated` set keeps the row honest while the server prop is stale. Refuted: the scratch-team roster-invite "dead code" (that IS the intended mutual exclusion) and a suspected stale-closure regression (pre-diff code was identical). Verified-correct: server-side ownership unbypassable by the second entry point, the shared catalog's `import type` keeps the service-role client out of the client bundle, activation never was plan-gated, no new route → no SW denylist change, dismiss localStorage key byte-identical (no coach loses an existing dismissal). ✅ **3 follow-ups OWNER-DECIDED 2026-07-30 — 2 built, 1 accepted as-is.** **(a) Lost-update race FIXED, ships mig 211** (owner: fix it now): activation was a read-modify-write in app code, so two activations for one team inside each other's read window (one coach, phone+laptop or two tabs) lost one silently; the merge now happens inside ONE `UPDATE` in `set_basic_coach_team_feature`, so callers serialise under the row lock. **Verified against the LIVE dev DB, not just typecheck** — anon gets `permission denied` (an open RPC would let anyone flip features on any team), unknown id raises instead of no-op'ing, add/re-add/remove correct, and **two concurrent activations of different features both survive** (the actual bug, reproduced green). **(b) Card-flash FIXED** (owner: use the existing pre-paint mechanism): worse than first reported — a coach who skips never completes the steps, so the card painted and collapsed on **every** Overview load *forever*, for exactly the people who opted out; now the root layout's existing `NO_FLASH_SCRIPT` (the one already stopping theme/density flash) reads the team id from the path and stamps `data-coach-setup-skipped` pre-paint, with a CSS gate — no cookie, no DB, no migration — and **`CoachPortalShell` strips the attribute on hydration + every pathname change** so a stale flag can't hide the card on the next team opened. **(c) Direct links to un-activated tool pages ACCEPTED AS-IS** — the panel is honest about it now and Explore is one tab away; auto-activating would grow the nav without the coach choosing (contradicting the opt-in model), and a read-only mode for four editors is disproportionate. Don't re-open without a new owner decision. ⚠⚠ **RELEASE GATE — mig 211 is DEV-ONLY, apply to prod BEFORE promoting, and `npm run check:migrations` gives a FALSE GREEN here: the snapshots track tables/columns/indexes/constraints/RLS but NOT functions, so the drift checker is blind to a missing function** (same class as the FK-action-drift gotcha — that check is not a parity gate). Failure mode if promoted without it: RPC errors → route 500s → coach sees "Could not update your team tools" and the tool never turns on (fail-closed and visible, but broken). New FAQ `faq-tool-used-but-no-tab` ("I added things to my Schedule, so why isn't there a Schedule tab?") — nothing is lost, names both ways to turn it on, explains why that step still says "Turn on". ~25 new search terms. Retiring an award type never deletes it or its history. `/review` (5-lens high-risk funnel): no Critical/High findings; 6 confirmed small issues fixed in-tree (an inline-created award type not refreshing the parent list until reload, a missing trophy badge on the mobile day-sheet view, a defensive modal-state reset on event switch, a cancelled game still being awardable if it had stale scores, a silent delete-failure with no error shown, and a DB-layer belt-and-suspenders guard against an award ever carrying both a game link and a tournament label) — 2 other flagged concerns checked directly and ruled out (a suspected timezone/date-shift bug, refuted against this exact page's own existing date-handling convention; and a comment-syntax claim raised independently by two reviewers, refuted against the real file). `/docs` synced: new Awards content + 2 FAQs in the game-day-details guide, the player-profile FAQ updated to mention awards, and a new leaderboard FAQ added next to the existing tags FAQ. typecheck + focused lint clean throughout; dev server restarted (shared-module changes) and verified live. **Owner live-tested 2026-07-12, 2 UX corrections applied:** save now closes the modal (was stay-open-and-reset); "Manage award types" removed from the schedule page (once-a-season task, doesn't belong on every game) — lives only on the "Who's earning it?" report page. ## 🧭 Deferred Enhancements (Confirmed scope, build later)

- [ ] **F4 — Trial checkpoint emails (League + Club)** — League orgs on a 30-day trial receive setup check-in emails at day 7, day 21, and ~day 27 (3-day warning already handled by Stripe `trial_will_end` webhook). Club orgs on a 90-day trial receive check-ins at day 7, day 30, day 60, and day 80. These are proactive activation emails (not just payment warnings). Implementation requires: a daily cron route at `/api/cron/trial-emails` protected by a shared secret, called by AWS EventBridge Scheduler; a `trial_email_sent` bitmask column on `organizations` for idempotency; and 4–5 email templates in the Resend stack. **Blocked on:** decision to enable EventBridge Scheduler in the Amplify account. See [STRIPE_INTEGRATION_PLAN.md](docs/projects/archive/STRIPE_INTEGRATION_PLAN.md) Phase F.

- [ ] **House League — Coach Draft Room** — Shareable per-team link (no login required) that lets coaches participate in the draft live. Each team gets a token-scoped URL; coaches see the current pick state and submit their pick when it's their turn. Requires: real-time state sync (polling or WebSocket), a `draftTokens` map in `draft_state`, and a public-facing draft room page. See conversation context from Phase 5G planning. **Architecture note:** Phase 5G is designed to not block this — draft business logic is kept auth-layer-agnostic so a token path can be added to `/draft/route.ts` without restructuring the state machine.
- [ ] **House League — Practice Scheduling** — Allow league admins to schedule practices for individual teams alongside the game schedule. A practice belongs to one team (not two), has no score, and does not affect standings. Confirmed scope post-Phase 5H. Build as an extension of the schedule page: separate "Practices" tab or filter, same date/time/location fields, team selector instead of home/away. No schema migration needed — can reuse `league_games` with `away_team_id = null` and a `game_type` column, or use a separate `league_practices` table (decide at build time).
- [ ] **Coaches Portal — Practice Plans** (Player Development roadmap Phase 4) — help a coach plan Tuesday's practice around who's working on what. Parked 2026-07-17 with a reserved hub slot; that slot was retired by the Development hub restructure (2026-07-31), so the plan re-argues placement on its merits. **PLANNING COMPLETE 2026-07-31 — awaiting owner ratification of D1–D10 + a mockup round.** Recommended anchor = the practice event in Schedule (D1b), with a pointer line in Development. ⚠ **D10 added after planning** (owner deferred it here from the Development hub): editable evaluation-session date + a link to the practice it happened at — the "wrote it down Tuesday, typed it Thursday" case; §10.1 carries the trap (readings are date-stamped at entry, so a date change must re-stamp them) and **round-1 mockups do NOT cover it — round 2 required before build**. (see docs/projects/archive/COACH_PRACTICE_PLANS_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md + _KICKOFF_PROMPT.md)
- [ ] **Calendar Sync for Team Schedules** — Allow parents/coaches to export a team's game schedule as an `.ics` file or subscribe via a calendar URL (Google/Apple Calendar). Technically straightforward (generate `.ics` from schedule query). Applies to both house league team schedules and rep team schedules in the coaches portal. Build during a parent-facing polish pass.
- [ ] **Bulk Operations for Admins** — Bulk-change registration statuses, bulk-assign teams, bulk-edit schedule slots. High value for orgs with 100+ registrations. Requires multi-select UI, confirmation flows, and async batch API routes. Revisit after Phase 3 UX ships and an org is operating at scale.
- [ ] **Tournament setup templates** - Post-clone enhancement for orgs that run several similar tournaments and want reusable setup patterns without choosing a prior event each time.
- [ ] **Tournament staff role presets** - Later enhancement for Plus orgs if real usage shows registrar, scheduler, communications, and scorer-manager presets would reduce setup mistakes beyond current role/capability overrides.
- [ ] **Custom domain investigation** — Research feasibility and effort of allowing orgs to point a custom domain (e.g. miltonbats.com) to their FieldLogicHQ public page. Covers: DNS verification flow, wildcard SSL or per-org cert provisioning, reverse proxy / Amplify routing changes, and potential upsell pricing. Do not design or implement until `module_public_site` is fully shipped. **Also investigate:** pros and cons of a custom domain for the Stripe Customer Portal (e.g. `billing.fieldlogichq.ca`) — level of effort, DNS setup, whether it meaningfully improves trust vs. billing.stripe.com, and when this becomes worth prioritizing.
- [ ] **Public Site Offering Evaluation** — After the first external org enables `module_public_site`, review the offering across three dimensions: (1) ease of setup — is the path from enabling the module to a live page clear enough for a non-technical org owner? (2) customization level — structured fields are correct; assess whether anything is missing without going full CMS; (3) base UX improvements. Produce a prioritized fix list before moving to the next module.

- [x] **🧮 Coach Budget Plan — totals, expected funding, period columns (✅ OWNER QA PASSED 2026-08-13)** — four owner findings on one screen. The competing *Total Planned Budget* / *Season Total* tiles became a **summary ladder** that shows its working (line items → the un-itemized part of an optional *Estimated total* → total planned → less expected funding → per player); a budget line can now be **expected funding** (fundraising, sponsorship, grant) so dues are generated off what players actually fund; the plan gained its own **month / quarter columns** (they existed only on Budget vs. Actual); and the duplicate foot link went. ### Coach budget line — period split rework (2026-08-12)
- [x] **🗓 Coach budget line — period split rework (✅ OWNER QA PASSED 2026-08-12)** — three owner-reported defects on the Add/Edit Budget Line modal, built to an approved 4-round mockup. (1) A save that couldn't go through printed its reason into a strip at the bottom of a *scrolling* body, so it was usually off-screen and the button read as broken; now a failed save **scrolls to the offending row, focuses it, marks it, and shows a counter in the sticky footer** that clicks back to the first problem — deliberately not a second dialog (the form already stacks a discard guard, and a dialog still can't say which of twelve rows is wrong). (2) **Period labels are no longer mandatory** — a period with a month/quarter/date names itself ("Apr 2027"), shown greyed in the field before it's saved and overwritable; the client resolves the name on save so the NOT-NULL column and the API contract are untouched. Dates are optional too (undated rows carry a visible advisory that Budget vs. Actual can't place them). Only money can block a save now. (3) **A line is split by months / quarters / specific dates / just names** — each row carries its own picker FIRST and the optional label second, "+ Add period" auto-advances the month so twelve taps + Split evenly builds a year, plus "Fill the season" and "Clear all periods". ⚠ **Changing the split mode RESETS the periods** (owner ruling — converting 12 months onto 4 quarters piled 3 rows per quarter and mapping back produced three Januaries; months→dates is lossless and deliberately NOT carved out). Bulk actions offer **Undo instead of a confirm dialog**. No migration, no API change, no new fetch — the month/quarter selection *is* the period's date, so BvA / month grid / installments are untouched; a saved line's mode is re-derived on open. New pure module + 28 unit tests (1636/1636 green); typecheck ✓ · verify:changed all green ✓. (see docs/projects/archive/COACH_BUDGET_PERIOD_SPLIT_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)

### Dev server memory ceiling made real + sweep watchdogs (2026-08-11)
- [x] **The dev server's memory cap had never been in force** — `package.json` asked for 3 GB on the command line, but `next dev` reads that limit from the `NODE_OPTIONS` environment variable only, discards a command-line one, and substitutes **half of installed RAM** (11182 MB here). The flag was inert for as long as it had existed. It cost two crashes read as "heavy sweep" rather than "no ceiling": 2026-08-02 (26 of 112 layout screens unmeasured) and 2026-08-11 (10.31 GB working set, machine at 1.7 GB free, dev server killed mid-sweep and again on auto-restart). `npm run dev` now routes through a launcher that sets the ceiling where Next actually looks — **owner-set 6 GB**, `DEV_HEAP_MB` to override — and warns if the number ever stops being below Next's own default. Proven both directions against Next's own resolver. `amplify.yml` was always correct and is untouched.
- [x] **Both browser-driving sweeps got a memory watchdog** — the layout sweep and the mobile capture refuse to start without headroom, abort mid-run if free memory falls through the floor (`DEV_FREE_FLOOR_MB`, default 1536 MB), and report their low-water mark on a clean finish. An abort exits non-zero **before** anything is written: a sweep that stopped early has unmeasured screens, and a baseline written from one records the product as cleaner than it is. Same reasoning as the existing refuse-partial-run guard, which an abort would otherwise slip past unnoticed.
- [x] **The dev server now recovers by itself** — the leak below means it dies during ordinary portal testing, and `next dev` exits outright rather than reviving, so the site silently stopped answering until someone noticed and restarted by hand. The launcher now supervises it and relaunches on the out-of-memory abort, saying loudly which restart number this is so the frequency stays visible instead of becoming background noise. Guarded against boot-loops (3 failures inside 15s of starting = a startup problem, not the leak — it stops and says so) and it never blames the leak for a crash that wasn't one.
- [x] **Root cause identified — it is Next's own development runtime, not our code** — measured over the inspector with forced GCs: ~2.7 MB retained per request with no plateau, ~126 of ~144 MB of surviving bytes traced to Next's dev-only runtime, our compiled chunks ~1 MB, and no unbounded accumulators anywhere in our code. **Not a sweep-only problem: ~21 coach pages of ordinary clicking exhausts it.** (2026-08-12 re-measure on shipped 16.3.0: the per-COMPILE half proved fixed; the per-request drip did not — open vercel/next.js#85666 — so the supervisor stays. See the completed upgrade entry above.)
- [x] **Written down as a standing rule** (AGENTS.md, beside the restart rules) — the dev server never releases a compiled route, so any full sweep leaves it permanently large and is itself a restart trigger; and the inert-flag shape is called out by name so a future cleanup pass cannot quietly reintroduce it.

### Stale tournament-slot cap fix — migration 121 (2026-06-10)
*(Bug: a Tournament Plus org saw "Your plan allows 3 tournament slots" when moving a tournament to draft. Root cause = stale `organizations.tournament_limit = 3` left over from when Tournament Plus was capped at 3 ([migration 037](supabase/migrations/037_tournament_plan_limits.sql)); after the cap was raised to unlimited (9999) in `lib/plan-config.ts`, `getEffectiveTournamentLimit()` still honors a stored finite cap verbatim for unlimited-tier plans — the intended platform-admin override path — so the stale value kept enforcing. Fix = data migration `121_lift_stale_unlimited_tournament_caps.sql` raising stored caps to 9999 for tournament_plus/league/club where `< 9999`, **excluding `[UAT_PROTECTED]` fixtures** (uat-plus-org/uat-club-org deliberately at 10). Applied dev (`dev-test-org` 3→9999; UAT fixtures preserved) + prod (0 rows — already clean, idempotent backstop). Data-only change: no schema/dictionary/snapshot impact. No code change — honoring a stored finite cap on unlimited tiers is the intended override mechanism.)*

### Release guardrail — prod migration safety (2026-06-09)
*(Migrations are never auto-applied by the release process — Amplify only builds, the `/release` agent only pushes git — so a dev-applied/prod-missing migration would 500 in prod, e.g. the migration-040 incident. Two guardrails added, local tooling: (A) `scripts/apply-migration-api.mjs` now takes a `--dev`(default)/`--prod` target so applying to prod is a deliberate one-liner; (B) new `scripts/check-prod-migration-drift.mjs` / `npm run check:migrations` queries both live DBs and exits 1 when prod is behind dev — wired into the `/release` agent preflight + promote gate + a hard rule. Verified: the check correctly blocks today on `basic_coach_team_players` (mig 114, dev-only).)*

### Moved from the open sections — 2026-08-10 status sweep

All items below were already checked complete but had never been moved here (the 2026-08-10 docs audit). Text preserved verbatim as the historical record.

- [x] **🖩 Scorekeeper score entry — mobile repair (2026-08-08, ✅ **DONE — LIVE on prod (2026-08-10 release, job 251)**; owner QA passed twice; ✅ `/review` run — 6 findings, all confirmed, 5 fixed; ✅ owner ruling: **three ways out became one** — the backdrop no longer dismisses (it was the only exit that could bin a half-typed score by accident, and with the keypad up it is a ~30px sliver) and the ✕ is retired, so **Cancel is the only way out, with Escape as its keyboard twin**; ⚠ **committed inside `3aeda5fd`, a concurrent agent's docs commit** — its own commit never ran, so `git log` will not lead anyone to this change; the plan doc is the record)** — the volunteer's score box computed to **~29px wide on every phone** (the −/+ steppers took 109 of the 138px each team gets), so the digit being typed could not be shown; the away score looked absent because it was. Steppers removed on an owner ruling — the sheet saves once and its own copy says the score is final immediately, so there was never a running tally to step. Also: the number pad now actually appears (the old input type let iOS and installed-PWA keyboards raise the full alphabet, and let a laptop scroll wheel change a final score), tapping a score selects it (correcting 5→3 gave 53), the keyboard's action key can no longer finalize, the header names the **game** (time · field · division) instead of repeating the team names printed below it, and the sheet now measures against the space the keypad leaves — Finalize previously cleared it by 5px. Mockups approved (artifact `f4bedded-…`, three options + a live keypad test). ⚠ **`check:layout` cannot see this screen** (no scorekeeper route in the sweep — it is behind a volunteer login); width claims are arithmetic, not rendered measurement, and closing that gap is the standing follow-up. Two UAT specs updated in the same change. (see docs/projects/archive/SCOREKEEPER_SCORE_ENTRY_MOBILE_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)

- [x] **📻 Day-of volunteer bottom bars — Option C (2026-08-07, ✅ COMPLETE — owner QA PASSED; committed `96c8e346`, LIVE on prod (2026-08-07 release))** — QA = ledger Group 3C / §7, all nine parts; **6 defects found and fixed in the run** (4 fixture, 2 feature: a clipped date, a duplicate calendar icon, the browser's Clear button stranding the board, and a public-site row that dead-ended at a private club and was then removed outright). ⚠ Left OPEN: the gate shell now has no public door at all — the original top-nav D9 finding resurfacing, deliberately NOT recorded as decided. — the scorekeeper and gate check-in screens gain a pinned status-bucket row plus a Score/Gate/Account tab bar on phones; the header sheds the hop link and Sign Out below 640px (both stay on desktop); the scorekeeper's three counter tiles retire (the bar carries the same numbers) and its date/search/field/division fold behind one Filters button, so games are visible on arrival instead of a screenful of setup. Account is a sheet holding identity + duties, install-app, the public site and sign out. Chrome only — no capability, route or API change. Also settles the ⇄ asymmetry the top-nav audit left open (D9): the public door lives in Account on both shells, and the "considered and declined" comment that was never actually a ruling is corrected in place. Bar heights are a declared budget every consumer composes from (the lineup-builder Undo-bar lesson). ⚠ The rendered layout sweep does not cover either shell at all (zero references — its list is coach-portal-only), so its 44px-tap-floor and trapped-under-chrome rules — exactly what fixed bottom chrome breaks — are NOT checking this; owner QA on a real phone is. ⚠ Desktop deviation: the volunteer gate board's arrival filter becomes a 4-across row at every width, matching its twin; the admin gate screen is untouched. (see docs/projects/archive/DAY_OF_VOLUNTEER_BOTTOM_BARS_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)

- [x] **Date Correctness — "what day is it?" in the org timezone** — ✅ **COMPLETE 2026-07-26.** Production runs UTC while orgs schedule in Toronto, so every calendar-day answer ("is it game day?", "is this overdue?", "days until") was **a day late from ~8 PM Toronto every evening** — and invisible on a dev box, which runs Toronto time. Symptoms: tournaments flipping to *finished* mid-event on closing night, dues/payables flagging overdue 4–5h early, out-of-province coaches seeing a different date than the organiser. **76 sites / 59 files fixed**, four duplicate overdue checks collapsed into one, and a **guardrail at a ZERO baseline** (pre-commit + deploy) so it cannot return. Also unlocked unit-testing of non-leaf modules and added the missing `npm test` script (391/391 pass). See [plan](docs/projects/archive/DATE_CORRECTNESS_PLAN.md) + [PM brief](docs/projects/archive/DATE_CORRECTNESS_PM_BRIEF.md).

- [x] **Schema Parity — dev↔prod (48 divergences → 0)** — Stage 1 ✅ 2026-07-27: parity gate shipped (`check:parity`, offline, ratcheted, wired into verify + deploy) after discovering `check:migrations` compares **existence only** and cannot see defaults/nullability/constraints/indexes. ⚠ **Found + fixed a LIVE PROD DATA-LOSS bug**: deleting a team CASCADE-deleted every game it played (scores included) because dev and prod had the same-named FK with opposite behaviour — mig 200 applied dev+prod, plus a 409 guard on team delete. Drift originates from the 9 tables hand-built per-environment BEFORE migrations existed (Apr–May 2026); nothing migration-created has drifted since. **✅ COMPLETE 2026-07-27 — 51 → 0.** Stages 2–4 (migs 201/202/203) applied to **dev AND prod**; `check:parity` baseline is now an **empty object**, so any future divergence fails the build immediately. Live-prod audit came back clean — **0 NULLs, 0 orphans, no backfill needed**. Found **3 more live prod failures** (deleting a venue, adding an address-less venue, importing a blank coach column all fail on prod but pass on dev) and a **second unguarded data-loss path**: deleting a division CASCADEs away its games + scores, while its confirmation dialog claimed the opposite — now a 409 `DIVISION_HAS_GAMES` guard + corrected copy. - [x] **Notification Center Rework** (owner-flagged 2026-07-06 via `/ux`) — ✅ **SHIPPED TO PROD 2026-07-06 (promote `58eec6f0`) — TODO said "unpushed" for 18 days; trued up 2026-07-24 by the cleanup audit (G22); plan + brief archived.** P4 adds the **"See all" full notifications page** (`/{org}/admin/notifications` + coaches equivalent, reached from a new "See all" link in the bell footer): full-width Needs-attention/Activity zones + bundling, an Unread/All toggle (defaults to All — the archive view), zone filter chips, Mark-all-read, and Load-more pagination; shared display logic extracted so the page + dropdown never drift; no by-tournament filter (rows carry no tournament id). P3 takes chat off the bell entirely (messages + @mentions) so it's pure operations — the Chat tab already carried its own unread badge on both shells, so this just removes the redundant bell entries (fixing today's chat double-count); @mentions still reach users via Chat-tab badge + phone push. Bell list + unread count exclude chat from one shared source (no badge/panel drift). Verified live against seed data; typecheck + verify:changed GREEN; dev restarted clean. P2 adds an **"Unread" toggle** (on by default → the bell reads as an inbox you empty; flip to "All" to see read items dimmed) and **same-type bundling** (repeated items roll up to "5 new registrations" / "2 payments received"; one tap marks all read + opens the list; needs-attention + chat never bundle). typecheck + verify:changed GREEN; HMR-safe. Seed script refreshed so both bundles show on Battle of the Bats by default. P1 (bell restructure): the bell now leads with a pinned **"Needs attention"** section (only the items needing a decision — failed payment, coach access request, disputed score, roster request, tryout response, assistant approval, no-show — amber-accented, with a count, that clears as you handle each) above an **Activity feed grouped Today / Yesterday / Earlier**. Category (act/know/talk) is a typecheck-enforced map so new event types can't skip classification. No bundling yet (P2), chat still in the bell (P2→P3). typecheck + verify:changed GREEN; HMR-safe (no restart). P0 quick win: every notification type now has a distinct icon (added the 8 missing ones) + fixed the phone "square" push icon — the status-bar badge was a fully-opaque tile that Android alpha-flattens to a white square; now a transparent centered white chevron silhouette (84.5% transparent, verified), plus a borderless corner-clean Android maskable icon. `/design` approved + logged the direction (own transparent badge source; branded per-notification icon flagged as a follow-up). Service-worker cache bumped so installed devices refresh. Focused lint clean. The bell is one time-sorted list that becomes clutter on a busy tournament day (repeated "New registration" rows bury the one item needing a decision); the phone push icon renders as a square. Owner picked the **Full Act / Know / Talk** scope: pin **needs-attention** items (failed payment, coach access request, disputed score) on top, turn the rest into a **bundled, date-grouped activity feed** ("6 new registrations" not six rows), **move chat off the bell** onto the Chat tab's own unread badge (reuses the existing `useChatUnread`/`ChatUnreadBadge` already on the coaches shell), add a **"See all"** overflow page, and **fix the phone icons** (the status-bar badge is a filled square → Android paints it solid white; needs a transparent chevron silhouette). **No migration** — category + bundling are code-derived; delivery engine (notify/prefs/mute) untouched. 5 shippable phases (P0 icons+icon-map quick win → P1 categorize → P2 bundle → P3 chat-off-bell → P4 See-all). Icon redraw routes to `/design`. (see docs/projects/archive/NOTIFICATION_CENTER_REWORK_PLAN.md + PM brief)
