Per-screen standings/teams skeletons NOT built — those pages are SSR'd with initialData (no client loading state) so a skeleton would be dead code. **Also finished (as-we-go fixes):** registration CTAs lifecycle+capacity aware (`lib/registration-state.ts` → nav "Register"/"Join Waitlist" hidden on live/full/complete), season picker removed (pending IA spec), nav centering (equal-flex sides), hero badge cleanup + nav-clearance fix. **Phase D started 2026-06-03**: **share-score cards BUILT** — primary share is a **LINK** (drives traffic) backed by a **dynamic OG image** (`schedule/[gameId]/opengraph-image.tsx`, Next `ImageResponse`/Satori, 1200×630, org-coloured, renders server-side at request time; verified 200 PNG) + per-game `generateMetadata` (og:title/twitter summary_large_image); `lib/share-card.ts` canvas 1080² PNG is the secondary **"Save image"**; `ShareScoreButton` (compact button → popover with Share link / Share image) on the game-detail rail + My-Team dock (followed team's latest result, live OR final). Also fixed NaNm dock countdown (DB `time` is HH:MM:SS → `.slice(0,5)`). **Share expanding to 4 surfaces** (schedule rows, champion/podium, team profile, tournament home): **tournament home + team profile SHIPPED 2026-06-03** — new `SharePageButton` (link-only) + a **per-route OG-image hierarchy** (`[tournamentSlug]/opengraph-image.tsx` event card = default for all tournament pages, `teams/[id]/opengraph-image.tsx` team card, `schedule/[gameId]/opengraph-image.tsx` score card) + tournament-layout og metadata; all verified 200 PNG. **Champion/podium + schedule-list rows PARKED (user deferred 2026-06-03 — home/team/game share deemed sufficient; schedule-rows would need a portal popover for `.gamesList` clipping).** **Desktop left-rail app shell BUILT 2026-06-03** (`components/public/TournamentSideRail.tsx`): desktop-only (≥1024px) FULL-HEIGHT vertical nav rail (z-index above top nav) w/ branded header (logo+name) + Overview/sections (active accent) + footer; content wrapper `.shell` `padding-left:248px`; top-bar `.links` + duplicate `.logo` hidden ≥1024px. **Desktop top-bar context header BUILT 2026-06-03** (`TournamentNavStatus.tsx`): left = phase-aware status pill + dates (Upcoming+countdown / In progress / Completed) → **live score ticker on game day** (polls `usePublicTournamentLive`); right = **Share event** button by Register; status data via new `setTournamentStatus` channel (OrgNavContext→TournamentNavSync→layout). A column-light, narrative-heavy phase. Built via 2 parallel extractors + a **FULL 3-agent adversarial pass** (kept ON — billing/Stripe = the high-risk class; **63 confirmed, 0 refuted, 6 nuance [0 blocking]** across all 3 verifiers; all 6 nuances folded as one-clause tightenings). **Live probes (dev+prod `information_schema`/`pg_trigger`/`pg_class`/`pg_policy`): `stripe_prices` has NO triggers; RLS ENABLED with ZERO policies; prod grants anon+authenticated SELECT (dev does not) — so RLS-no-policies is the only thing keeping it off prod's public REST API. Content probed: 24 rows/env, 6 plan_ids, mirror-imaged (dev maps sandbox rows, prod maps live rows).** Verified gotchas: the webhook derives the plan from the PRICE via `getPlanFromPriceId` (reverse, env-blind) not metadata — an unmapped `price_id` SILENTLY no-ops entitlement + isn't even logged (webhook doesn't import observability); environment = `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` prefix sniff (`sk_live_`→live else sandbox; NODE_ENV irrelevant), re-implemented 4+ places; CHECK value is `sandbox` not `test`; `organizations.subscription_status` can hold RAW Stripe statuses outside the 4-value TS type (org webhook writes `sub.status` verbatim; only team paths normalize) and every gate checks only `=== 'canceled'`; `invoice.payment_failed`→past_due only (full suspension/retention cascade is `subscription.deleted`-only); downgrade/cancel are DB-FIRST (Stripe reconciliation failure → `billing_stripe_reconciliation_failed` audit + contact-support 500, DB already changed); 3 Stripe-bypass paths (mock billing [prod-hard-false], no-key direct-apply, founding-season comp) write plan state with NO Stripe object; UPDATE-only table (no code INSERT; seed-only via migs 048+065); every `price_id` write requires an APPROVED catalog change request; `last_change_note`/`product_name` write-status documented (note overwrite-with-NULL, never exercised live). Negative-test re-proved column-granular enforcement live (80 sealed). **Phase 8 (Platform admin) BUILT 2026-06-10 (pinned commit `cbcf7c7`; snapshot 2026-06-10, dev 113 / prod 113; all 17 tables column/CHECK/UNIQUE/FK-identical dev/prod, ZERO structural drift):** +17 tables documented & SEALED under a new `# Domain: Platform admin` heading (after Stripe/Billing, before Observability) across 3 labeled sub-domains (`## Platform identity, RBAC & audit` · `## Plans & catalog control plane` · `## Platform ops & data import`) → **97 tables sealed; Platform-admin control plane COMPLETE** (platform_users, platform_audit_log, platform_user_notes, platform_admin_visits, platform_events; plan_gating, plan_config_overrides, platform_plan_versions, platform_plan_module_entitlements, platform_addon_catalog, platform_catalog_change_requests/_change_applications/_campaigns; platform_bulk_operations, platform_metric_snapshots, import_batches, import_batch_rows; 171 cols = 139 documented + 32 boilerplate waivers). Built via 5 parallel extractors + a **FULL 4-agent adversarial pass** (kept ON — RBAC/billing-governance/audit class; 167 claims confirmed, 1 blocking + ~9 minor/precision fixes folded; blocking = the `platform_admin`/`founding_season` event sources ARE written, not dormant). **Live probe (dev+prod `information_schema`/`pg_class`/`pg_policies`/`pg_trigger`/`role_table_grants`): all 17 RLS-ENABLED, ZERO policies, ZERO triggers; prod grants anon+authenticated full CRUD on 16 of 17 (all but `plan_gating`=service_role-only), dev only REFERENCES/TRIGGER/TRUNCATE — RLS-no-policies is the ONLY thing keeping this control plane off prod's public REST API.** Verified gotchas: RBAC bootstrap = the `PLATFORM_ADMIN_EMAILS` env allowlist (super_admin, no DB row → explains prod platform_users=0); 6-role CHECK incl. `growth`, unknown→read_only fail-safe; **runtime-source split** — only `plan_gating` (checkout gating via getPlanGatingMap) + `plan_config_overrides` (checkout-time limits) are runtime-consumed, while `platform_plan_module_entitlements`/`_plan_versions`/`addon_catalog`/`catalog_campaigns` are admin-display/planning catalogs NOT read at runtime (hasModuleEntitlement uses static PLAN_CONFIG); catalog-governance gate authorizes by `proposal.kind` not `request_type`, NO separation of duties (same manage_product submits+approves), `_change_applications` ledger best-effort (swallows errors) → `platform_audit_log` is the durable trail; `platform_events` = business-event log (31-value union, only 4 read) DISTINCT from Observability errors (app-side dedup, TOCTOU, no unique constraint); 5 built-but-unused tables (user_notes/bulk_operations/metric_snapshots/catalog_campaigns 0 rows, plan_versions 1 seed row no writer); import_batches/_rows are ORG-scoped (not platform-admin). Negative-test re-proved column-granular enforcement live (97 sealed). **Phase 9 (CRM, 1 tbl `early_access_leads`) + Phase 10 (Notifications & Push, 8 tbl across 3 sub-systems: in-app bell+prefs / web push / email) BUILT 2026-06-10 (pinned commit `9faea936`; snapshot 2026-06-10, dev 113 / prod 113; all 9 tables column/constraint/FK-identical, ZERO structural drift, ZERO triggers + ZERO CHECKs):** two new `# Domain:` headings inserted between Platform admin and Observability → **106 tables sealed; CRM + Notifications domains COMPLETE** (only `tournament_roster_players` left acknowledged). 4 parallel extractors + a FULL 4-agent adversarial pass (35 confirmed / 1 refuted / 2 nuance, all folded). **Live RLS probe refuted two assumptions:** only `notifications` carries RLS policies (2 own-row select/update, VESTIGIAL — every data path is service-role), the other 8 are RLS-enabled-zero-policies; `early_access_leads` has NO anon-INSERT policy (service-role lead capture). Verified gotchas: early_access DUAL-STATUS trap (`internal_status` live, `status` dead; conversion keys off `converted_org_id`, not the status string); `tournament_notification_preferences` = per-USER opt-out (not contact-member `notify_mode`); `notify()` fan-out hub (5 dead event types; name-collides-but-disjoint from `platform_events`); two push tables by design (both EMPTY dev+prod; member vs anonymous-fan identity); `platform_email_templates` is a MIRROR (`resolveEmailTemplate` never built — hardcoded `lib/email.ts` sends); NO `scheduled_at` DB column (Resend native param). Negative-test re-proved column-granular enforcement (106 sealed). **Observability & Feedback COLUMN-SEAL pass BUILT 2026-06-10** (atop commit `412e4036`; the Phase-3 feedback center + `request_id` Mechanism-A are uncommitted working-tree — refs working-tree-relative): the 6 Observability tables upgraded table-granular → **column-sealed** (67 anchors) → **112 tables sealed; ALL 11 domains column-sealed except the deferred `tournament_roster_players`**. 3 extractors + a 2-agent adversarial pass (17 confirmed / 0 refuted / 3 nuance, all folded). All 6 are RLS-enabled-zero-policies-zero-triggers (these DO have CHECKs); `record_error_event` is plain plpgsql (NOT security definer) while the fold/sweep ARE; `error_groups` has no org_id (2-hop org_slug filter); `occurrence_count` counts all but events are sampled; env CHECK on groups not events; `request_id` Mechanism-A = the bug→error deep-link; metrics instrument only 2 routes; `feedback_submissions` brand-new (0 rows) with many write-only admin-trail cols. Closes the residual "added a migration but forgot to refresh+commit the snapshot" hole the coverage ratchet can't see. **`tournament_roster_players` COLUMN-SEALED 2026-06-11 — the FINAL table → 113/113 tables column-sealed, `acknowledgedTables` EMPTY, the Data Dictionary is content-COMPLETE across all 11 domains.** (`source_player_id` back-link added free-tier Phase 5j, mig 123, FK ON DELETE SET NULL, dev-only; the 3 deferred notes were already flipped.)
